## 06YU2 `skewer`

**Full:** Mistake by White: allowed a skewer. Middlegame. White is ahead by 1 point of material. White has an isolated a-pawn, an isolated c-pawn and an isolated h-pawn. Black has an isolated a-pawn, an isolated c-pawn, an isolated e-pawn and a passed e-pawn. The b-, d- and f-files are open. White has half-open e- and g-files. White's king is exposed, away from its back rank. Black's king is on the kingside with a weakened pawn shield. Black to move.

**Short:** Mistake by White: allowed a skewer. Middlegame.

## 0THBB `hangingPiece`

**Full:** Mistake by White: left a piece undefended. Middlegame. White is ahead by 6 points of material. White has a passed a-pawn and a passed b-pawn. Black has a passed e-pawn. The c- and d-files are open. White has a half-open e-file. Black has half-open a- and b-files. White's king is on the kingside with a weakened pawn shield. Black's king is exposed, away from its back rank. Black to move.

**Short:** Mistake by White: left a piece undefended. Middlegame.
